\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\beta,\bar{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{2}\alpha^{F}\rangle|\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\sigma,\nabla(D_{\mu}R_{4\nu}-D_{\nu}R_{4\mu})\epsilon^{\mu\nu}~_{34}\rangle|

\displaystyle\sim|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\sigma,\nabla(\widehat{\nabla}(\alpha^{F}\cdot\rho^{F}-\alpha^{F}\cdot\sigma^{F})-\chi(\rho^{F}\rho^{F}+\alpha^{F}\bar{\alpha}^{F}+\sigma^{F}\sigma^{F}+(\eta-\underline{\eta})\alpha^{F}(\sigma^{F}+\rho^{F}))\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\rho,\nabla(D_{3}R_{44}-D_{4}R_{34})\rangle\sim\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\rho,\widehat{\nabla}(\alpha^{F}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\alpha^{F}-\widehat{\nabla}\rho^{F}+~^{*}\widehat{\nabla}\sigma^{F}-2~^{*}\eta\sigma^{F}+2\eta\rho^{F}

\displaystyle+2\underline{\omega}\alpha^{F}-\widehat{\chi}\bar{\alpha}^{F})

\displaystyle-4\underline{\omega}|\alpha^{F}|^{2}-4\eta\alpha^{F}(\rho^{F}-\sigma^{F})+\rho^{F}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F}+2\sigma^{F}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F}-2\underline{\eta}\alpha^{F}(\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F}))\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})

where
\nabla\rho
and the
\widehat{\nabla}^{2}(\alpha^{F},\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})
have to be bounded in
L^{2}(H)
, the remaining algebraic terms can be bounded in
L^{\infty}(S)
and then apply Sobolev embedding for the
L^{\infty}(S)
factor i.e.,
||\alpha^{F},\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}||_{L^{\infty}(S)}\leq C(\mathcal{O}_{0})||\widehat{\nabla}(\alpha^{F},\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{4}(S)}
.
There will be term of type
|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\nabla\Psi\bar{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{2}\bar{\alpha}^{F}|
which may be a cause of concern. However, since we have
L^{4}(S)
estimate for
\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F}
, we can control it in
L^{\infty}(S)
through Sobolev embedding. Therefore we obtain

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\nabla\Psi\bar{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{2}\bar{\alpha}^{F}|=|\int_{0}^{u}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{S}\nabla\Psi\bar{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{2}\bar{\alpha}^{F}|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\bar{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{\infty}}||\nabla\Psi||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}})}||\widehat{\nabla}^{2}\bar{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})

where
\Psi
is the Weyl curvature component belonging to the set
(\alpha,\beta,\rho,\sigma)
. In other words, as long as we do not have bad terms like
\nabla\bar{\alpha}\bar{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{2}\bar{\alpha}^{F}
, we will always have a gain of the factor
\epsilon
. Thus

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\sigma,\nabla(D_{\mu}R_{3\nu}-D_{\nu}R_{3\mu})\epsilon^{\mu\nu}~_{34})\rangle\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

(7.69)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\rho,\nabla(D_{3}R_{44}-D_{4}R_{33})\rangle\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

(7.70)

and

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\bar{\beta},\nabla(D_{b}R_{43}-D_{3}R_{4b})\rangle\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}).

(7.71)

Putting all the terms together, we obtain

\displaystyle|ER12|\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W}_{0},\mathcal{F}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})

(7.72)

and therefore

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u}}|\nabla\beta|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}}|\nabla\rho|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}}|\nabla\sigma|^{2}=\int_{H_{0}}|\nabla\beta|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}}|\nabla\rho|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}}|\nabla\sigma|^{2}

(7.73)

\displaystyle+\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W}_{0},\mathcal{F}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})\leq C(\mathcal{W}_{0})+\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})

Proceeding in an exactly similar way, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{\underline{H}_{u}}|\nabla\bar{\beta}|^{2}+\int_{H_{u}}|\nabla\rho|^{2}+\int_{H_{u}}|\nabla\sigma|^{2}\leq C(\mathcal{W}_{0})+\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}).

(7.74)

Now we focus on the most dangerous term which is the term arising from the remaining pair
(\bar{\alpha},\bar{\beta})

\displaystyle 2\int_{H_{u}}|\nabla\bar{\beta}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}}|\nabla\bar{\alpha}|^{2}=2\int_{H_{0}}|\nabla\bar{\beta}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}}|\nabla\bar{\alpha}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}2|\nabla\bar{\beta}|^{2}(2\underline{\omega}-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}|\nabla\bar{\alpha}|^{2}(2\omega-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi)}_{ER13}

\displaystyle-\underbrace{2\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\left(2\langle\nabla\bar{\beta},\nabla_{3}\nabla\bar{\beta}\rangle+\langle\nabla\bar{\alpha},\nabla_{4}\nabla\bar{\alpha}\rangle\right)}_{ER14}.

Now note the most important fact about the structure of the Yang-Mills sourced null Bianchi equations. In the term
ER_{13}
we encounter the term
\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}|\nabla\bar{\alpha}|^{2}(2\omega-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi)
which could potentially be dangerous since we can not touch
|\nabla\bar{\alpha}|^{2}
term. But luckily, the connection coefficients
(\omega,\mbox{tr}\chi)
that multiply
|\nabla\bar{\alpha}|^{2}
satisfy
\nabla_{3}
evolution equation and therefore are solely estimated by means of the initial data
\mathcal{O}_{0}
. This crucial fact will allow us to make use of the Grönwall’s inequality.
ER13
is estimated as

\displaystyle|ER13|\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})+C(\mathcal{O}_{0})\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||\nabla\bar{\alpha}||^{2}_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}.

(7.75)

For the term
ER14
, we need caution since
\nabla\bar{\alpha}
is involved. We explicitly evaluate
ER14
using the commuted evolution equations

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\bar{\beta},\nabla_{3}\nabla\bar{\beta}\rangle+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\bar{\alpha},\nabla_{4}\nabla\bar{\alpha}\rangle

(7.76)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}2\langle\nabla\bar{\beta},-2\nabla(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\bar{\beta})-div\nabla\bar{\alpha}-2\nabla(\underline{\omega}\bar{\beta})+\nabla(\underline{\eta}\cdot\bar{\alpha})+\frac{1}{2}\nabla(D_{b}R_{33}-D_{3}R_{3b})

\displaystyle+[\nabla_{3},\nabla]\bar{\beta}-[\nabla,div]\bar{\alpha}\rangle

(7.77)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\bar{\alpha},-\frac{1}{2}\nabla(\mbox{tr}\chi\bar{\alpha})-\nabla\hat{\otimes}\nabla\bar{\beta}+4\nabla(\omega\bar{\alpha})-3\nabla(\underline{\widehat{\chi}}\rho-~^{*}\underline{\widehat{\chi}}\sigma)+\nabla((\zeta-4\underline{\eta})\hat{\otimes}\bar{\beta})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\nabla(D_{4}R_{33}-D_{3}R_{34})\gamma+[\nabla_{4},\nabla]\bar{\alpha}+[\nabla,\nabla]\hat{\otimes}\bar{\beta}\rangle

\displaystyle\sim\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\bar{\beta},-2\nabla(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\bar{\beta})-2\nabla(\underline{\omega}\bar{\beta})+\nabla(\underline{\eta}\cdot\bar{\alpha})+\frac{1}{2}\nabla(D_{b}R_{33}-D_{3}R_{3b})

\displaystyle+(\bar{\beta}+\bar{\alpha}^{F}(\sigma^{F}+\rho^{F}))\bar{\beta}+(\eta+\underline{\eta})\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}-\bar{\chi}\nabla\bar{\beta}+\chi\underline{\eta}\bar{\beta}-K\bar{\alpha}\rangle

(7.78)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\bar{\alpha},-\frac{1}{2}\nabla(\mbox{tr}\chi\bar{\alpha})+4\nabla(\omega\bar{\alpha})-3\nabla(\underline{\widehat{\chi}}\rho-~^{*}\underline{\widehat{\chi}}\sigma)+\nabla((\zeta-4\underline{\eta})\hat{\otimes}\bar{\beta})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\nabla(D_{4}R_{33}-D_{3}R_{34})\gamma+(\beta+\alpha^{F}(\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F}))\bar{\alpha}+(\eta+\underline{\eta})\nabla_{4}\bar{\alpha}-\chi\nabla\bar{\alpha}+\chi\underline{\eta}\bar{\alpha}+K\bar{\beta}\rangle

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle(\eta+\underline{\eta})\nabla\bar{\beta},\nabla\bar{\alpha}\rangle,

where once again the principal terms cancel point-wise after an integration by parts procedure. Apart from the cancellation of the principal terms, note that the
\gamma-
trace-less property of
\bar{\alpha}
removes the term
\langle\nabla\bar{\alpha},\nabla(D_{4}R_{33}-D_{3}R_{34})\gamma\rangle
. This essentially indicates the fact that
\bar{\alpha}
does not interact with the source stress-energy tensor at the level of topmost derivative. In order to estimate the remaining terms, we need to explicitly write down the Yang-Mills source term

\displaystyle\nabla(D_{b}R_{33}-D_{3}R_{3b})\sim\nabla(\langle\bar{\alpha}^{F},\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F}\rangle-\bar{\chi}\bar{\alpha}^{F}\cdot(\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F})+\underline{\eta}|\bar{\alpha}^{F}|^{2}-2\underline{\omega}\bar{\alpha}^{F}\cdot(\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle-\widehat{\nabla}_{3}(\bar{\alpha}^{F}\cdot\rho^{F}+\bar{\alpha}^{F}\cdot\sigma^{F}))

\displaystyle\sim\langle\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F},\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F}\rangle+\langle\bar{\alpha}^{F},\widehat{\nabla}\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F}\rangle-\nabla(\bar{\chi}\bar{\alpha}^{F}(\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F}))+\underline{\eta}\langle\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F},\bar{\alpha}^{F}\rangle-\nabla(\underline{\omega}\bar{\alpha}^{F}(\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F}))

\displaystyle-\widehat{\nabla}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\bar{\alpha}^{F}(\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F})-\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\bar{\alpha}^{F}(\widehat{\nabla}\rho^{F}+\widehat{\nabla}\sigma^{F}),

where we notice that the potentially dangerous terms
\widehat{\nabla}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\bar{\alpha}^{F}
and
\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\bar{\alpha}^{F}
appear. However, these terms can be controlled by
\mathcal{F}
. We have
||\nabla_{3}\alpha^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}
estimated in terms of
\mathcal{F}
by means of the co-dimension 1 trace inequality (
6.153
) and
||\widehat{\nabla}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\bar{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}
is estimated in terms of
\mathcal{F}
. We first write down how to control these two terms

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\bar{\beta},\widehat{\nabla}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\bar{\alpha}^{F}(\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F})\rangle|\leq||\nabla\bar{\beta}||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}})}||\nabla\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\bar{\alpha}^{F}(\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}})}

(7.79)

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{u}||\nabla\bar{\beta}||_{L^{2}(H)}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}(||\widehat{\nabla}\rho^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}+||\widehat{\nabla}\sigma^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)})\sup_{\underline{u}}||\widehat{\nabla}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\bar{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}\leq\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\bar{\beta},\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})|\leq\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{u}||\nabla\bar{\beta}||_{L^{2}(H)}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\bar{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\widehat{\nabla}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{4}(S)}

(7.80)

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})

where we have utilized the fact that
\nabla\bar{\beta}
is controlled on
H
(in addition to
\underline{H}
). In addition, we also have terms of the following types

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\bar{\beta},\widehat{\nabla}^{2}\bar{\alpha}^{F}\bar{\alpha}^{F}\rangle|\leq\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{u}||\nabla\bar{\beta}||_{L^{2}(H)}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\widehat{\nabla}\alpha||_{L^{4}(S)}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\widehat{\nabla}^{2}\bar{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}

(7.81)

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\bar{\beta},\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F}\rangle|\leq\epsilon\sup_{u}||\nabla\bar{\beta}||_{L^{2}(H)}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F}||^{2}_{L^{4}(S)}

(7.82)

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\bar{\beta},\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F}\varphi\rangle|\leq\epsilon\sup_{u}||\nabla\bar{\beta}||_{L^{2}(H)}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\varphi||_{L^{\infty}(S)}

(7.83)

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\bar{\beta},\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\bar{\alpha}^{F}\varphi\rangle|\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

(7.84)

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\bar{\alpha},\alpha^{F}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\bar{\alpha}\rangle|\leq\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}||\nabla\bar{\alpha}||_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\alpha^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}||\widehat{\nabla}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{4}(S)}||\bar{\alpha}||_{L^{4}(S)}\leq

(7.85)

\displaystyle\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})

Therefore the coupled Yang-Mills-Einstein terms are under control. Now we control the remaining terms using the estimates on the connection coefficients (lemma
6.1
-
6.7
) as follows

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\bar{\beta},\bar{\beta}\nabla\varphi\rangle|\leq\epsilon\sup_{u}||\nabla\bar{\beta}||_{L^{2}(H)}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\bar{\beta}||_{L^{4}(S)}||\nabla\varphi||_{L^{4}(S)}\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

(7.86)

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\bar{\beta},\nabla\bar{\beta}\varphi\rangle|\leq\epsilon\sup_{u}||\nabla\bar{\beta}||^{2}_{L^{2}(H)}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\varphi||_{L^{\infty}(S)}\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

(7.87)

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\bar{\beta},\nabla\bar{\alpha}\varphi\rangle|\leq\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{u}||\nabla\bar{\beta}||_{L^{2}(H)}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\nabla\bar{\alpha}||_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\varphi||_{L^{\infty}(S)}\leq\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

(7.88)

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\bar{\beta},K\bar{\alpha}\rangle|\leq\epsilon\sup_{u}||\nabla\bar{\beta}||_{L^{2}(H)}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||K||_{L^{4}(S)}||\bar{\alpha}||_{L^{4}(S)}\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}).

(7.89)

Now we estimate the most dangerous terms

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\bar{\alpha},\nabla\bar{\alpha}\mbox{tr}\chi\rangle|\leq\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\mbox{tr}\chi||_{L^{\infty}(S)}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||\nabla\bar{\alpha}||^{2}_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}\leq C(\mathcal{O}_{0})\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||\nabla\bar{\alpha}||^{2}_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}},

(7.90)

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\bar{\alpha},\nabla\bar{\alpha}\omega\rangle|\leq\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\omega||_{L^{\infty}(S)}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||\nabla\bar{\alpha}||^{2}_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}\leq C(\mathcal{O}_{0})\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||\nabla\bar{\alpha}||^{2}_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}},

(7.91)

where we notice that the connection coefficients that appear multiplied with the top derivative of
\bar{\alpha}
satisfy
\nabla_{3}
equation and therefore solely determined by the initial data
\mathcal{O}_{0}
. This allows us to use the Grönwall’s inequality to complete the energy estimate. The remaining terms are harmless

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\bar{\alpha},\nabla\Psi\varphi\rangle|\leq\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),~|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\bar{\alpha},\nabla\varphi\Psi\rangle|\leq\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

(7.92)

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle(\eta+\underline{\eta})\nabla\bar{\beta},\nabla{\bar{\alpha}}\rangle|\leq\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

(7.93)

where
\Psi\in(\beta,\bar{\beta},\rho,\sigma,\alpha)
and
\varphi
is any connection coefficients belonging to the set
(\widehat{\chi},\underline{\widehat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\chi,\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\underline{\eta},\omega,\eta,\underline{\omega})
. Collecting all the terms, we may now estimate
ER14
as follows

\displaystyle|ER14|\leq\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})+\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})+C(\mathcal{O}_{0})\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||\nabla\bar{\alpha}||^{2}_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}.

(7.94)

Therefore collecting all the terms we obtain

\displaystyle 2\int_{H_{u}}|\nabla\bar{\beta}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}}|\nabla\bar{\alpha}|^{2}\leq 2\int_{H_{0}}|\nabla\bar{\beta}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}}|\nabla\bar{\alpha}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})+\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})+C(\mathcal{O}_{0})\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||\nabla\bar{\alpha}||^{2}_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\mathcal{F}_{0})+\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})+\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})+C(\mathcal{O}_{0})\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||\nabla\bar{\alpha}||^{2}_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

This completes the proof of the lemma.

\square

7.2
Energy estimates for the Yang-Mills curvature

Now that we have completed the energy estimates for the Weyl curvature, we move on to estimating the energy associated with the Yang-Mills curvature. For this purpose, we could use the canonical stress-energy tensor associated with the Yang-Mills theory. However, we will follow the direct integration by parts procedure using the null Yang-Mills equations. In the Yang-Mills case, we will not do the energy estimates separately for each order but only for the topmost derivatives. If the estimates at the top order close, so do the lower orders since the hyperbolic structure of the null Yang-Mills equations is preserved after commuting with derivatives.

Lemma 7.3

The horizontal derivatives of the null Yang-Mills curvature satisfy the following
L^{2}
energy estimates

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F}|^{2}\leq C(\mathcal{F}_{0})+\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

(7.95)

\displaystyle\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\bar{\alpha}^{F}|^{2}+\int_{H_{u}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}|^{2}+\int_{H_{u}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F}|^{2}

(7.96)

\displaystyle\leq C(\mathcal{W}_{0})+\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})+\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})+C(\mathcal{O}_{0})\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F}||^{2}_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

for
0\leq I\leq 2
,
I\in\mathbb{Z}
.

Proof.
In order to prove this estimate, we use the integration identities (
7.1
-
7.2
) once again. First, we identify the pairs
(\alpha^{F},\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})
and apply the identities (
7.1
-
7.2
) to obtain

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F}|^{2}=\int_{H_{0}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F}|^{2}

(7.97)

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F}|^{2}(2\underline{\omega}-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}|^{2}(2\omega-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi)+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F}|^{2}(2\omega-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi)}_{ER15}

\displaystyle-\underbrace{2\int_{D_{u},\underline{u}}\left(\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F}\rangle+\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}\rangle+\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F}\rangle\right)}_{ER16}.

We can directly estimate
ER15
by using the estimates of the connection coefficients (lemma
6.1
) and the definition of
\mathcal{F}
since all the Yang-Mills curvature coefficients can be estimated on
H
by

\displaystyle|ER15|\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}).

(7.98)

In order to estimate
ER16
, we use the null Yang-Mills equations

\displaystyle ER16=-2\int_{D_{u},\underline{u}}\left(\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F}\rangle+\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}\rangle+\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F}\rangle\right)

(7.99)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\left(\langle\nabla^{I}\alpha^{F},-\frac{1}{2}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\alpha^{F})-\widehat{\nabla}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}+~^{*}\widehat{\nabla}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F}-2\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(~^{*}\underline{\eta}\sigma^{F})+2\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\underline{\eta}\rho^{F})\right.

\displaystyle\left.+2\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\underline{\omega}\alpha^{F})-\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\widehat{\chi}\cdot\bar{\alpha}^{F})+[\widehat{\nabla}_{3},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}]\alpha^{F}+[\widehat{\nabla},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}]\rho^{F}-[^{*}\widehat{\nabla},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}]\sigma^{F}\rangle\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F},-\hat{div}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F}-\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\mbox{tr}\chi\rho^{F})-\widehat{\nabla}^{I}((\eta-\underline{\eta})\cdot\alpha^{F})+[\widehat{\nabla}_{4},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}]\rho^{F}+[\hat{div},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}]\alpha^{F}\rangle\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F},-\hat{curl}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F}-\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\mbox{tr}\chi\sigma^{F})+\widehat{\nabla}^{I}((\eta-\underline{\eta})\cdot~^{*}\alpha^{F})+[\widehat{\nabla}_{4},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}]\sigma^{F}-[\hat{curl},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}]\alpha^{F}\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\sim\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\left(\langle\nabla^{I}\alpha^{F},-\frac{1}{2}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\alpha^{F})-2\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(~^{*}\underline{\eta}\sigma^{F})+2\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\underline{\eta}\rho^{F})\right.

\displaystyle\left.+2\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\underline{\omega}\alpha^{F})-\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\widehat{\chi}\cdot\bar{\alpha}^{F})+[\widehat{\nabla}_{3},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}]\alpha^{F}+[\widehat{\nabla},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}]\rho^{F}-[^{*}\widehat{\nabla},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}]\sigma^{F}\rangle\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F},-\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\mbox{tr}\chi\rho^{F})-\widehat{\nabla}^{I}((\eta-\underline{\eta})\cdot\alpha^{F})+[\widehat{\nabla}_{4},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}]\rho^{F}+[\hat{div},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}]\alpha^{F}\rangle\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F},-\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\mbox{tr}\chi\sigma^{F})+\widehat{\nabla}^{I}((\eta-\underline{\eta})\cdot~^{*}\alpha^{F})+[\widehat{\nabla}_{4},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}]\sigma^{F}-[\hat{curl},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}]\alpha^{F}\rangle\right)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F},((\eta+\underline{\eta})(\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}+\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F})\rangle.

We will sketch how we handle each term. There will be terms of the following type that are estimated easily

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F},\varphi\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F}\rangle\leq\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{2}(S)}||\varphi||_{L^{\infty}(S)}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{2}(S)}\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

(7.100)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\varphi\Phi^{F}\rangle\leq\int_{0}^{u}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{2}(H)}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\varphi||_{L^{2}(H)}||\Phi^{F}||_{L^{\infty}(S)}\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

(7.101)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F},\widehat{\nabla}\varphi\widehat{\nabla}\Psi\rangle\leq\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{2}(S)}||\widehat{\nabla}\varphi||_{L^{4}(S)}||\widehat{\nabla}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

where
\Phi^{F}\in(\alpha^{F},\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})
and therefore are controllable on
H
. For the connection coefficients, we use the lemma (
6.1
-
6.7
). Now we need to control the commutator terms

\displaystyle[\widehat{\nabla}_{3},\widehat{\nabla}^{2}]\alpha^{F}=[\widehat{\nabla}_{3},\widehat{\nabla}]\widehat{\nabla}\alpha^{F}+\widehat{\nabla}[\widehat{\nabla}_{3},\widehat{\nabla}]\alpha^{F}

(7.102)

Now

\displaystyle[\widehat{\nabla}_{3},\widehat{\nabla}]\Phi^{F}\sim\bar{\beta}\Phi^{F}+\bar{\alpha}^{F}\Phi^{F}+\bar{\alpha}^{F}(\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F})\Phi^{F}+(\eta+\underline{\eta})\nabla_{3}\Phi^{F}-\bar{\chi}\nabla\Phi^{F}+\bar{\chi}\eta\Phi^{F},

and therefore

\displaystyle[\widehat{\nabla}_{3},\widehat{\nabla}]\widehat{\nabla}\alpha^{F}\sim\bar{\beta}\widehat{\nabla}\alpha^{F}+\bar{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}\alpha^{F}+\bar{\alpha}^{F}(\rho^{F}-\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}\alpha^{F}+(\eta+\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\widehat{\nabla}\alpha^{F}

(7.103)

\displaystyle-\bar{\chi}\nabla\widehat{\nabla}\alpha^{F}+\bar{\chi}\eta\widehat{\nabla}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle\sim\bar{\beta}\widehat{\nabla}\alpha^{F}+\bar{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}\alpha^{F}+\bar{\alpha}^{F}(\rho^{F}-\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}\alpha^{F}+(\eta+\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle-\bar{\chi}\nabla\widehat{\nabla}\alpha^{F}+\bar{\chi}\eta\widehat{\nabla}\alpha^{F}+(\eta+\underline{\eta})[\widehat{\nabla}_{3},\widehat{\nabla}]\alpha^{F},

where
\widehat{\nabla}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\alpha^{F}
is evaluated by means of the null Yang-Mills equations. Now

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F},[\widehat{\nabla}_{3},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}]\Phi^{F}\rangle

(7.104)

would contain the following terms

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F},\bar{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}\Phi^{F}\rangle|\leq\int_{u,\underline{u}}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{2}(S)}||\bar{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}||\widehat{\nabla}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{F}),

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F},\bar{\alpha}^{F}(\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}\alpha^{F}\rangle|\leq\int_{u,\underline{u}}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{2}(S)}||\bar{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}||\widehat{\nabla}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}||\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{\infty}(S)}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u,\underline{u}}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{2}(S)}||\bar{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}||\widehat{\nabla}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}||\widehat{\nabla}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{4}(S)}\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{F}),

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F},\varphi\varphi\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F}\rangle|\leq\int_{u,\underline{u}}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{2}(S)}||\varphi||_{L^{\infty}(S)}||\varphi||_{L^{4}(S)}||\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F},\varphi\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F}\rangle|\leq\int_{u,\underline{u}}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F}||^{2}_{L^{2}(S)}||\varphi||_{L^{\infty}(S)}\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

(7.105)

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F}\bar{\beta}\widehat{\nabla}\Phi^{F}|\leq\int_{u,\underline{u}}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{2}(S)}||\bar{\beta}||_{L^{4}(S)}||\widehat{\nabla}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})

Now note the following commutation relation

\displaystyle[\widehat{\nabla}_{4},\widehat{\nabla}^{2}](\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})=[\widehat{\nabla}_{4},\widehat{\nabla}]\widehat{\nabla}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\widehat{\nabla}[\widehat{\nabla}_{4},\widehat{\nabla}](\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

(7.106)

Now

\displaystyle[\widehat{\nabla}_{4},\widehat{\nabla}](\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\sim\beta(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\alpha^{F}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\alpha^{F}(\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F})(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+(\eta+\underline{\eta})\nabla_{4}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle-\chi\nabla(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\chi\underline{\eta}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}),

and therefore

\displaystyle[\widehat{\nabla}_{4},\widehat{\nabla}]\widehat{\nabla}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\sim\beta\widehat{\nabla}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\alpha^{F}(\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+(\eta+\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\widehat{\nabla}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})-\chi\nabla\widehat{\nabla}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\bar{\chi}\eta\widehat{\nabla}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle\sim\beta\widehat{\nabla}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+{\alpha}^{F}(\rho^{F}-\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+(\eta+\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})-\bar{\chi}\nabla\widehat{\nabla}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+\bar{\chi}\eta\widehat{\nabla}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+(\eta+\underline{\eta})\left(\beta+\alpha^{F}(\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F}))(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+(\eta+\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}_{4}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\chi\widehat{\nabla}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\chi\underline{\eta}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\right),

where
\widehat{\nabla}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})
can be further simplified by means of the null Yang-Mills equations. The resulting terms are easy to estimate

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F},\Phi^{F}\widehat{\nabla}\Phi^{F}\rangle\leq\int_{u,\underline{u}}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{2}(S)}||\Phi^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}||\widehat{\nabla}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{F}),

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F},\Phi^{F}(\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}\Phi^{F}\rangle\leq\int_{u,\underline{u}}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{2}(S)}||\Phi^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}||\widehat{\nabla}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}||(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{\infty}(S)}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u,\underline{u}}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{2}(S)}||\Phi^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}||\widehat{\nabla}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}||\widehat{\nabla}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{4}(S)}\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{F}),

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F},\varphi\varphi\widehat{\nabla}\Phi^{F}\rangle\leq\int_{u,\underline{u}}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{2}(S)}||\varphi||_{L^{\infty}(S)}||\varphi||_{L^{4}(S)}||\widehat{\nabla}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F},\varphi\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F}\rangle\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

where once again
\Phi^{F}\in(\alpha^{F},\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})
and
\varphi
is any connection coefficients belonging to the set
(\widehat{\chi},\underline{\widehat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\chi,\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\underline{\eta},\omega,\eta,\underline{\omega})
.
Now note the following

\displaystyle[\widehat{\nabla},\widehat{\nabla}^{2}]\Phi^{F}=\widehat{\nabla}[\widehat{\nabla},\widehat{\nabla}]\Phi^{F}+[\widehat{\nabla},\widehat{\nabla}]\widehat{\nabla}\Phi^{F}\sim\widehat{\nabla}((K+\sigma^{F})\Phi^{F})+(K+\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}\Psi

(7.107)

and therefore

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F},[\widehat{\nabla},\widehat{\nabla}^{2}]\Phi^{F}\rangle|\sim|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F},\widehat{\nabla}((K+\sigma^{F})\Phi^{F})+(K+\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}\Phi^{F}\rangle|

(7.108)

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u,\underline{u}}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{2}(S)}\left(||\nabla K||_{L^{2}(S)}||\Phi^{F}||_{L^{\infty}(S)}+||K||_{L^{4}(S)}||\widehat{\nabla}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}+||\widehat{\nabla}\sigma^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}||\Phi^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+||\sigma^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}||\widehat{\nabla}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}\right)\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})

where
||\nabla K||_{L^{2}(S)},~||K||_{L^{4}(S)}
are controlled by
\mathcal{W}
and
\mathcal{F}
by the virtue of the null Hamiltonian constraint (
3.33
, corollary 1). Collecting all the terms together, we obtain

\displaystyle|ER16|\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})

(7.109)

and therefore

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F}|^{2}=\int_{H_{0}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F}|^{2}

(7.110)

\displaystyle+\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})\leq C(\mathcal{W}_{0})+\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}).

Now in order to prove the second estimate of the lemma, we collect the triple
(\bar{\alpha},\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})
and apply the integration identities (
7.1
-
7.2
)

\displaystyle\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\bar{\alpha}^{F}|^{2}+\int_{H_{u}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}|^{2}+\int_{H_{u}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F}|^{2}=\int_{\underline{H}_{0}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\bar{\alpha}^{F}|^{2}+\int_{H_{0}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}|^{2}+\int_{H_{0}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F}|^{2}

(7.111)

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\bar{\alpha}^{F}|^{2}(2\omega-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi)+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}|^{2}(2\underline{\omega}-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F}|^{2}(2\underline{\omega}-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})}_{ER17}

\displaystyle-2\underbrace{\int_{D_{u},\underline{u}}\left(\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\bar{\alpha}^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\bar{\alpha}^{F}\rangle+\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}\rangle+\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F}\rangle\right)}_{ER18}.

Once again notice that the term involving
|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\bar{\alpha}^{F}|^{2}
contains connection coefficients
(\omega,\mbox{tr}\chi)
which satisfy
\nabla_{3}
evolution equations and therefore are estimated solely by means of the initial data allowing us to use Grönwall’s inequality.
ER17
is estimated a follows

\displaystyle|ER17|\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})+C(\mathcal{O}_{0})\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\bar{\alpha}^{F}||^{2}_{L^{2}(\bar{H)}}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}.

(7.112)

Once again
ER18
is controlled by means of commuted null Yang-Mills equations

\displaystyle\int_{D_{u},\underline{u}}\left(\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\bar{\alpha}^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\bar{\alpha}^{F}\rangle+\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}\rangle+\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F}\rangle\right)

(7.113)

\displaystyle\sim\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\left(\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\bar{\alpha}^{F},-\frac{1}{2}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\mbox{tr}\chi\bar{\alpha}^{F})-2\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(~^{*}\underline{\eta}\cdot\sigma^{F})-2\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\underline{\eta}\cdot\rho^{F})+2\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\omega\bar{\alpha}^{F})-\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\underline{\widehat{\chi}}\cdot\alpha^{F})\rangle\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\rho^{F})+\widehat{\nabla}^{I}((\eta-\underline{\eta})\cdot\bar{\alpha}^{F})\rangle+\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F},-\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\sigma^{F})+\widehat{\nabla}^{I}((\eta-\underline{\eta})\cdot~^{*}\bar{\alpha}^{F})\rangle\right)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\bar{\alpha}^{F},[\widehat{\nabla}_{4},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}]\bar{\alpha}^{F}\rangle+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\bar{\alpha}^{F},[\widehat{\nabla},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}](\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\rangle+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F},[\widehat{\nabla}_{3},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}]\rho^{F}\rangle

(7.114)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F},[\widehat{\nabla},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}]\bar{\alpha}^{F}\rangle+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F},[\widehat{\nabla},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}]\bar{\alpha}^{F}\rangle+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F},((\eta+\underline{\eta})(\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}+\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F})\rangle

The terms that appear with the top order derivative of
\bar{\alpha}^{F}
are
\mbox{tr}\chi,\omega,
and
\underline{\widehat{\chi}}
which are determined solely by
\mathcal{O}_{0}
. We use the following commutation relation

\displaystyle[\widehat{\nabla}_{4},\widehat{\nabla}]\bar{\alpha}^{F}\sim\beta\bar{\alpha}^{F}+\alpha^{F}\bar{\alpha}^{F}+\alpha^{F}(\rho^{F}-\sigma^{F})\bar{\alpha}^{F}+(\eta+\underline{\eta})\nabla_{4}\bar{\alpha}^{F}-\bar{\chi}\nabla\bar{\alpha}^{F}+\chi\underline{\eta}\bar{\alpha}^{F}

to evaluate
[\widehat{\nabla}_{4},\widehat{\nabla}^{2}]\bar{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle[\widehat{\nabla}_{4},\widehat{\nabla}^{2}]\bar{\alpha}^{F}=[\widehat{\nabla}_{4},\widehat{\nabla}]\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F}+\widehat{\nabla}[\widehat{\nabla}_{4},\widehat{\nabla}]\bar{\alpha}^{F}.

(7.115)

These will contain terms of the type

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\bar{\alpha}^{F},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\varphi\bar{\alpha}^{F}\rangle\leq||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\bar{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}})}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}||\widehat{\nabla}\varphi||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\epsilon^{1/2}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{F}),

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\bar{\alpha}^{F},\widehat{\nabla}\varphi\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F}\rangle\leq\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\bar{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}(S)}||\widehat{\nabla}\varphi||_{L^{4}(S)}||\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}\leq\epsilon^{1/2}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{F})

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F},\nabla K\bar{\alpha}^{F}\rangle\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{F}),~\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F},K\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F}\rangle\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{F})

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\bar{\alpha}^{F},K\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F}\rangle\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{F}),~\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F},\nabla K\bar{\alpha}^{F}\rangle\leq\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{F}),

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F},\varphi\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F}\rangle\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{F}),~\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\bar{\alpha}^{F},\nabla\varphi\bar{\alpha}^{F}\rangle\leq\epsilon^{1/2}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{F})

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\bar{\alpha}^{F},(\omega,\mbox{tr}\chi)\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\bar{\alpha}^{F}\rangle\leq C(\mathcal{O}_{0})\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\bar{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}(\underline{H})},

Notice that the last term is controllable by Grönwall’s inequality and connection coefficients in the last integral do not contain
\eta
and
\underline{\omega}
. Therefore, collecting all the terms, we obtain

\displaystyle|ER18|\leq\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})+\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})+C(\mathcal{O}_{0})\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F}||^{2}_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}.

(7.116)

Substituting this estimate in the main energy identity (
7.111
) we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\bar{\alpha}^{F}|^{2}+\int_{H_{u}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}|^{2}+\int_{H_{u}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F}|^{2}=\int_{\underline{H}_{0}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\bar{\alpha}^{F}|^{2}+\int_{H_{0}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}|^{2}+\int_{H_{0}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\sigma^{F}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})+\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})+C(\mathcal{O}_{0})\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F}||^{2}_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\mathcal{F}_{0})+\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})+\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})+C(\mathcal{O}_{0})\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F}||^{2}_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

This completes the proof of the lemma.

\square

7.3
Energy estimates for
\nabla_{4}\alpha,\nabla_{3}\underline{\alpha}

We need to estimate the remaining terms of the curvature energy.

Lemma 7.4

The null derivatives of the null components of the Weyl curvature satisfy the following
L^{2}
energy estimates

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u}}|\nabla_{4}\alpha|^{2}\leq C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W}_{0},\mathcal{F}_{0})+\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0}\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

(7.117)

\displaystyle\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}}|\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha}|^{2}\leq C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W}_{0},\mathcal{F}_{0})+\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0}\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})+\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})

(7.118)

\displaystyle+C(\mathcal{O}_{0})\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha}||^{2}_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

Proof.
We proceed exactly the same way as the previous case only we commute the null derivatives with the evolution equations of the Weyl curvature components. First we identify the set of pairs
(\alpha,\beta)
and
(\bar{\alpha},\bar{\beta})
and apply the integration identities (
7.1
-
7.2
)

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u}}|\nabla_{4}\alpha|^{2}+2\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}}|\nabla_{4}\beta|^{2}=\int_{H_{0}}|\nabla_{4}\alpha|^{2}+2\int_{\underline{H}_{0}}|\nabla_{4}\beta|^{2}

(7.119)

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}|\nabla_{4}\alpha|^{2}(2\underline{\omega}-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}2|\nabla_{4}\beta|^{2}(2\omega-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi)}_{ER19}

\displaystyle-2\underbrace{\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\left(\langle\nabla_{4}\alpha,\nabla_{3}\nabla_{4}\alpha\rangle+2\langle\nabla_{4}\beta,\nabla_{4}\nabla_{4}\beta\rangle\right)}_{ER20}

Now notice that
\nabla_{4}\beta
contains the term
\nabla\alpha
which can not be controlled on
\underline{H}
and therefore we needed to include
||\nabla_{4}\beta||_{L^{2}(\underline{H}_{0}})
term in the initial data for the Weyl curvature. Fortunately,
\nabla_{4}\beta
appears in the error term
ER19
within the bulk integral multiplied by
\omega
and
\mbox{tr}\chi
and therefore harmless. We first estimate the error terms

\displaystyle|ER19|=|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}|\nabla_{4}\alpha|^{2}(2\underline{\omega}-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}2|\nabla_{4}\beta|^{2}(2\omega-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi)|

(7.120)

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})+C(\mathcal{O}_{0})\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||\nabla_{4}\beta||^{2}_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

where notice the connection coefficients multiplying
|\nabla_{4}\beta|^{2}
in the bulk integral is solely determined by the initial data
\mathcal{O}_{0}
. This allows us to utilize Grönwall’s estimate. Now for the term
ER20
, we need to use the commuted evolution equations

\displaystyle ER20=-2\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\left(\langle\nabla_{4}\alpha,\nabla_{3}\nabla_{4}\alpha\rangle+2\langle\nabla_{4}\beta,\nabla_{4}\nabla_{4}\beta\rangle\right)

(7.121)

\displaystyle=-2\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\left(\langle\nabla_{4}\alpha,\nabla\hat{\otimes}\nabla_{4}\beta-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{4}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\alpha)+4\nabla_{4}(\underline{\omega}\alpha)-3\nabla_{4}(\widehat{\chi}\rho+~^{*}\chi\sigma)+\nabla_{4}((\zeta+4\eta)\hat{\otimes}\beta)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{4}(D_{3}R_{44}-D_{4}R_{33})\gamma+[\nabla_{3},\nabla_{4}]\alpha+[\nabla_{4},\nabla]\hat{\otimes}\beta\rangle+2\langle\nabla_{4}\beta,div(\nabla_{4}\alpha)-2\nabla_{4}(\mbox{tr}\chi\beta)-2\nabla_{4}(\omega\beta)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\nabla_{4}(\eta\alpha)-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{4}(D_{b}R_{44}-D_{4}R_{4b})+[\nabla_{4},div]\alpha\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\sim\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\left(\langle\nabla_{4}\alpha,-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{4}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\alpha)+4\nabla_{4}(\underline{\omega}\alpha)-3\nabla_{4}(\widehat{\chi}\rho+~^{*}\chi\sigma)+\nabla_{4}((\eta+\underline{\eta})\hat{\otimes}\beta)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{4}(D_{3}R_{44}-D_{4}R_{33})\gamma+[\nabla_{3},\nabla_{4}]\alpha+[\nabla_{4},\nabla]\hat{\otimes}\beta\rangle+\langle\nabla_{4}\beta,-2\nabla_{4}(\mbox{tr}\chi\beta)-2\nabla_{4}(\omega\beta)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\nabla_{4}(\eta\alpha)-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{4}(D_{b}R_{44}-D_{4}R_{4b})+[\nabla_{4},div]\alpha\rangle\right)+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle(\eta+\underline{\eta})\nabla_{4}\beta,\nabla_{4}\alpha\rangle.

Now write down the commutators explicitly

\displaystyle[\nabla_{3},\nabla_{4}]\alpha\sim\sigma\alpha+(\rho^{F}\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F}\sigma^{F})\alpha+\omega\nabla_{3}\alpha+\underline{\omega}\nabla_{4}\alpha+(\eta-\underline{\eta})\nabla\alpha,

(7.122)

\displaystyle[\nabla_{4},\nabla]\beta\sim(\beta+\alpha^{F}(\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F}))\beta+(\eta+\underline{\eta})\nabla_{4}\beta-\chi\nabla\beta+\chi\underline{\eta}\beta,

\displaystyle[\nabla_{4},\nabla]\alpha\sim(\beta+\alpha^{F}(\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F}))\alpha+(\eta+\underline{\eta})\nabla_{4}\alpha-\chi\nabla\beta+\chi\underline{\eta}\alpha

and use the following null evolution equation for
\beta

\displaystyle\nabla_{4}\beta_{a}+2\mbox{tr}\chi\beta_{a}=(div\alpha)_{a}-2\omega\beta_{a}+(\eta\cdot\alpha)_{a}-\frac{1}{2}(\mathcal{D}_{a}R_{44}-\mathcal{D}_{4}R_{4a}),

(7.123)

where the Yang-Mills source term may be evaluated as follows

\displaystyle D_{b}R_{44}-D_{4}R_{4b}\sim\langle\alpha^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{b}\alpha^{F}\rangle-\chi_{bc}\mathfrak{T}_{c4}+\eta_{b}\mathfrak{T}_{44}-2\omega\mathfrak{T}_{4b}-(\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}_{b}\cdot(\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle-\alpha^{F}_{b}\cdot(\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F}+\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})).

In addition, we will use the null evolution equations for the connection coefficients whenever they are available. In the error term
ER20
, we note that
\nabla_{4}\underline{\eta}
appears. We can use the estimate from lemma
6.7
to control this term (this was the whole point of proving lemma
6.4
-
6.7
).
Note
\langle\nabla_{4}\alpha,\nabla_{4}(D_{3}R_{44}-D_{4}R_{33})\gamma\rangle=0
(since
\nabla_{4}
commutes with
\gamma
). Now we may estimate each term separately

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla_{4}\alpha,\nabla_{4}\varphi\alpha\rangle|\leq\epsilon\sup_{u}||\nabla_{4}\alpha||_{L^{2}(H)}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\nabla_{4}\varphi||_{L^{4}(S)}||\alpha||_{L^{4}(S)}\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

(7.124)

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla_{4}\alpha,\varphi\nabla_{4}\alpha\rangle|\leq\epsilon\sup_{u}||\nabla_{4}\alpha||^{2}_{L^{2}(H)}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\varphi||_{L^{\infty}(S)}\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla_{4}\alpha\varphi\nabla_{4}\Psi\rangle|\leq\epsilon\sup_{u}||\nabla_{4}\alpha||_{L^{2}(H)}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\varphi||_{L^{\infty}(S)}||\nabla_{4}\Psi||_{L^{2}(S)}\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla_{4}\alpha\nabla_{4}\varphi\Psi\rangle|\leq\epsilon\sup_{u}||\nabla_{4}\alpha||_{L^{2}(H)}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\nabla_{4}\varphi||_{L^{4}(S)}||\Psi||_{L^{4}(S)}\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla_{4}\alpha\varphi\nabla\Psi\rangle|\leq\epsilon\sup_{u}||\nabla_{4}\alpha||_{L^{2}(H)}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\varphi||_{L^{\infty}(S)}||\nabla\Psi||_{L^{2}(S)}\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\alpha,\varphi\Phi^{F}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}\rangle|\leq\epsilon\sup_{u}||\nabla\alpha||_{L^{2}(H)}\sup_{u}||\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}||_{L^{2}(H)}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\varphi||_{L^{\infty}(S)}||\widehat{\nabla}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\alpha,\varphi\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\Phi^{F}\rangle|\leq\epsilon\sup_{u}||\nabla\alpha||_{L^{2}(H)}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\varphi||_{L^{\infty(S)}}||\nabla\alpha^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}||\widehat{\nabla}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{2}(S)}

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla\alpha,\varphi\nabla_{4}\alpha\rangle|\leq\epsilon\sup_{u}||\nabla\alpha||_{L^{2}(H)}||\nabla_{4}\alpha||_{L^{2}(H)}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\varphi||_{L^{\infty}(S)}\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla_{4}\alpha,\varphi\Phi^{F}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}\rangle|\leq\epsilon\sup_{u}||\nabla_{4}\alpha||_{L^{2}(H)}\sup_{u}||\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}||_{L^{2}(H)}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\varphi||_{L^{\infty}(S)}||\widehat{\nabla}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla_{4}\alpha,\varphi\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\Phi^{F}\rangle|\leq\epsilon\sup_{u}||\nabla_{4}\alpha||_{L^{2}(H)}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\varphi||_{L^{\infty(S)}}||\nabla\alpha^{F}||_{L^{4}(S)}||\widehat{\nabla}\Phi^{F}||_{L^{2}(S)}

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

where
\Psi:(\alpha,\beta,\rho,\sigma)
and
\Phi^{F}:=(\alpha^{F},\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})
. We have also used the
||\nabla_{4}\varphi||_{L^{4}(S)}
estimate from corollary (2). Collecting all the terms, we obtain

\displaystyle|ER20|\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})

(7.125)

and therefore

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u}}|\nabla_{4}\alpha|^{2}+2\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}}|\nabla_{4}\beta|^{2}\leq\int_{H_{0}}|\nabla_{4}\alpha|^{2}+2\int_{\underline{H}_{0}}|\nabla_{4}\beta|^{2}

(7.126)

\displaystyle+\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})+C(\mathcal{O}_{0})\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||\nabla_{4}\beta||^{2}_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}\leq C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W}_{0},\mathcal{F}_{0})+\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})

by means of Grönwall’s inequality. Now we need to prove the second part of the lemma. Once again we identify the pair
(\bar{\alpha},\bar{\beta})
and apply the integration identities (
7.1
-
7.2
) to yield

\displaystyle 2\int_{H_{u}}|\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}}|\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha}|^{2}=2\int_{H_{0}}|\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}}|\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}2|\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}|^{2}(2\underline{\omega}-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}|\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha}|^{2}(2\omega-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi)}_{ER21}

\displaystyle-2\underbrace{\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\left(2\langle\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta},\nabla_{3}\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}\rangle+\langle\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha},\nabla_{4}\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha}\rangle\right)}_{ER22}.

We first control
ER21
. Notice that
\underline{\omega}
does not satisfy a
\nabla_{3}
equation and as a result
||\underline{\omega}||_{L^{\infty}(S)}\leq C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})
. Luckily
|\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}|^{2}
is controlled on
H
and therefore the first term of
ER21
can be estimated as
\int_{0}^{u}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})||\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}||^{2}_{L^{2}(H)}du^{{}^{\prime}}
. For the second term, once again
|\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha}|^{2}
is multiplied by
\omega
and
\mbox{tr}\chi
which are solely determined by the norm of the initial data
\mathcal{O}_{0}
. Therefore, we have

\displaystyle|ER21|\leq C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})\int_{0}^{u}||\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}||^{2}_{L^{2}(H)}du^{{}^{\prime}}+C(\mathcal{O}_{0})\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha}||^{2}_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

(7.127)

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})\sup_{u}||\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}||^{2}_{L^{2}(H)}+C(\mathcal{O}_{0})\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha}||^{2}_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

Utilizing the null Bianchi equations,
ER22
may be written as follows

\displaystyle ER22=-2\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\left(2\langle\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta},\nabla_{3}\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}\rangle+\langle\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha},\nabla_{4}\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha}\rangle\right)

\displaystyle=-2\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\left(2\langle\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta},-(div\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha})-2\nabla_{3}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\bar{\beta})-2\nabla_{3}(\underline{\omega}\bar{\beta})+\nabla_{3}(\underline{\eta}\cdot\bar{\alpha})\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{3}(\mathcal{D}_{a}R_{33}-\mathcal{D}_{3}R_{3a})+[div,\nabla_{3}]\bar{\alpha}\rangle\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\langle\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha},-(\nabla\hat{\otimes}\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta})-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{3}(\mbox{tr}\chi\bar{\alpha})+4\nabla_{3}(\omega\bar{\alpha})-3\nabla_{3}(\underline{\widehat{\chi}}\rho-~^{*}\underline{\widehat{\chi}}\sigma)+\nabla_{3}((\zeta-4\underline{\eta})\hat{\otimes}\bar{\beta})\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{3}(\mathcal{D}_{4}R_{33}-\mathcal{D}_{3}R_{34})\gamma+[\nabla_{4},\nabla_{3}]\bar{\alpha}+[\nabla,\nabla_{3}]\hat{\otimes}\bar{\beta}\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\sim\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\left(\langle\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta},-2\nabla_{3}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\bar{\beta})-2\nabla_{3}(\underline{\omega}\bar{\beta})+\nabla_{3}(\underline{\eta}\cdot\bar{\alpha})+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{3}(\mathcal{D}_{a}R_{33}-\mathcal{D}_{3}R_{3a})+[div,\nabla_{3}]\bar{\alpha}\rangle\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\langle\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha},-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{3}(\mbox{tr}\chi\bar{\alpha})+4\nabla_{3}(\omega\bar{\alpha})-3\nabla_{3}(\underline{\widehat{\chi}}\rho-~^{*}\underline{\widehat{\chi}}\sigma)+\nabla_{3}((\zeta-4\underline{\eta})\hat{\otimes}\bar{\beta})\right.

\displaystyle\left.+[\nabla_{4},\nabla_{3}]\bar{\alpha}+[\nabla,\nabla_{3}]\hat{\otimes}\bar{\beta}\rangle+\langle(\eta+\underline{\eta})\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta},\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha}\rangle\right),

where
\langle\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha},\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{3}(D_{4}R_{33}-D_{3}R_{44})\gamma\rangle=0
due to
\gamma-
trace-less property of
\bar{\alpha}
. Now we want to estimate each term separately. First, note the commutators property

\displaystyle[\nabla_{3},\nabla_{4}]\bar{\alpha}\sim\sigma\bar{\alpha}+(\rho^{F}\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F}\sigma^{F})\bar{\alpha}+\omega\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha}+\underline{\omega}\nabla_{4}\bar{\alpha}+(\eta-\underline{\eta})\nabla\bar{\alpha},

(7.128)

\displaystyle[\nabla_{3},\nabla]\bar{\beta}\sim(\bar{\beta}+\bar{\alpha}^{F}(\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F}))\bar{\beta}+(\eta+\underline{\eta})\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}-\bar{\chi}\nabla\bar{\beta}+\bar{\chi}\eta\bar{\beta},

\displaystyle[\nabla_{3},\nabla]\bar{\alpha}\sim(\bar{\beta}+\bar{\alpha}^{F}(\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F}))\bar{\alpha}+(\eta+\underline{\eta})\nabla_{4}\bar{\alpha}-\bar{\chi}\nabla\bar{\alpha}+\bar{\chi}\eta\bar{\alpha}.

Also, recall the null Bianchi equations that we shall make use of

\displaystyle\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}_{a}+2\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\bar{\beta}_{a}=-(div\bar{\alpha})_{a}-2\underline{\omega}\bar{\beta}_{a}+(\underline{\eta}\cdot\bar{\alpha})_{a}+\frac{1}{2}(\mathcal{D}_{a}R_{33}-\mathcal{D}_{3}R_{3a})

(7.129)

\displaystyle\nabla_{4}\bar{\alpha}_{ab}+\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi\bar{\alpha}=-(\nabla\hat{\otimes}\bar{\beta})_{ab}+4\omega\bar{\alpha}_{ab}-3(\underline{\widehat{\chi}}_{ab}\rho-~^{*}\underline{\widehat{\chi}}_{ab}\sigma)+((\zeta-4\underline{\eta})\hat{\otimes}\bar{\beta})_{ab}

(7.130)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}(\mathcal{D}_{4}R_{33}-\mathcal{D}_{3}R_{34})\gamma_{ab}+\frac{1}{2}\left(D_{a}R_{3b}+D_{b}R_{3a}\right),

and

(7.131)

\displaystyle-\widehat{\nabla}_{3}(\bar{\alpha}^{F}\cdot\rho^{F}+\bar{\alpha}^{F}\cdot\sigma^{F}).

Note an extremely important point that

\displaystyle||\nabla(\eta-\underline{\eta})||_{L^{4}(S)}\leq C\left(||\nabla(\eta-\underline{\eta})||_{L^{4}(S_{0,\underline{u}})}+||\nabla(\eta-\underline{\eta})||^{1/2}_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}||\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\nabla(\eta-\underline{\eta})||^{1/4}_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}(\nabla||\eta-\underline{\eta}||_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+||\nabla^{2}(\eta-\underline{\eta})||_{L^{2}(\underline{H})})^{1/4}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C(\mathcal{O}_{0})+\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F},\mathcal{W}(S),\mathcal{F}(S))

\displaystyle||\eta-\underline{\eta}||_{L^{\infty}(S)}\leq C(\mathcal{O}_{0}).

(7.134)

This is extremely important to handle the term

as follows

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta},(\nabla_{3}\varphi)\bar{\beta}|\leq\epsilon\sup_{u}||\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}||^{2}_{L^{2}(H)}du^{{}^{\prime}}+\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

(7.137)

(7.138)

(7.139)

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F}\Phi^{F}\rangle|\leq||\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}})}||\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\widehat{\nabla}\bar{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}})}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\Phi^{F}||_{L^{\infty}(S)}

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})\sup_{u}||\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}||_{L^{2}(H)}

(7.141)

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F}),

(7.143)

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta},\varphi\nabla_{4}\bar{\alpha}\rangle|\leq\epsilon\sup_{u}||\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}||_{L^{2}(H)}||\nabla_{4}\bar{\alpha}||_{L^{2}(H)}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\varphi||_{L^{\infty}(S)}

(7.144)

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})\sup_{u}||\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}||_{L^{2}(H)},

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta},\varphi\nabla\bar{\alpha}\rangle|\leq\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{u}||\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}||_{L^{2}(H)}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\nabla\bar{\alpha}||_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}||\varphi||_{L^{\infty}(S)}

(7.145)

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})\sup_{u}||\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}||_{L^{2}(H)},

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle(\eta+\underline{\eta})\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta},\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha}\rangle|\leq\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})\sup_{u}||\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}||_{L^{2}(H)},

(7.146)

where we have utilized the available Bianchi equations and null Yang-Mills equations (e.g., we have
\nabla_{4}\bar{\alpha}
which does not contain derivatives of
\bar{\alpha}
thereby allowing us to control it on
H
since we have
L^{4}(S)
of
\bar{\alpha}
under control). Once again, we observe that the connection coefficients
\omega
and
\mbox{tr}\chi
multiplying the most dangerous term
\langle\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha},\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha}\rangle
are determined completely by their initial value. Collecting all the terms, we obtain

\displaystyle 2\int_{H_{u}}|\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}}|\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha}|^{2}\leq 2\int_{H_{0}}|\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}}|\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha}|^{2}+\epsilon C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})\sup_{u}||\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}||^{2}_{L^{2}(H)}

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W},\mathcal{F})\sup_{u}||\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}||_{L^{2}(H)}+C(\mathcal{O}_{0})\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha}||^{2}_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}+C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W}_{0},\mathcal{F}_{0})\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha}||_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

which upon utilizing Grönwall’s inequality and smallness of
\epsilon
yields

\displaystyle 2\int_{H_{u}}|\nabla_{3}\bar{\beta}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}}|\nabla_{3}\bar{\alpha}|^{2}\leq C(\mathcal{O}_{0},\mathcal{W}_{0},\mathcal{F}_{0}).

(7.147)

This concludes the proof of the lemma.

\square

Lemma 7.5

The null derivatives of the null components of the Weyl curvature satisfy the following

(7.150)

for

.

Proof.
We only sketch the proof for

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}_{4}\alpha^{F}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}_{4}\rho^{F}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}_{4}\sigma^{F}|^{2}=\int_{H_{0}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}_{4}\alpha^{F}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}_{4}\rho^{F}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}_{4}\sigma^{F}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}_{4}\alpha^{F}|^{2}(2\underline{\omega}-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}_{4}\rho^{F}|^{2}(2\omega-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi)+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}_{4}\sigma^{F}|^{2}(2\omega-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi)}_{ER23}

\displaystyle-\underbrace{2\int_{D_{u},\underline{u}}\left(\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}_{4}\alpha^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}_{4}\alpha^{F}\rangle+\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}_{4}\rho^{F}\rangle+\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}_{4}\sigma^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}_{4}\sigma^{F}\rangle\right)}_{ER24}.

Using the estimates on the connection coefficients, the first error term

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}_{4}\alpha^{F},((\eta+\underline{\eta})(\widehat{\nabla}^{I}_{4}\rho^{F}+\widehat{\nabla}^{I}_{4}\sigma^{F})\rangle.

(7.154)

through Grönwall estimate and smallness of

. Exact same calculations but commuting the e.o.m with
